## Reviewer Guidelines

The recommended workflow is to (1) read the paper and get the first impression, and (2) search for related work. Reviewers should state in the review if they did not check some major parts, such as proofs, which helps the AC understand what aspects were not reviewed.

_source line 2698 · 2026-05-21T12:46:45.284359+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form Dimensions and Rubrics

## Primary Rating Dimensions

**Soundness** (1-5 scale with half-point increments)
- 5 = "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen"
- 4 = "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims"
- 3 = "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims"
- 2 = "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported"
- 1 = "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

**Excitement** (1-5 scale with half-point increments)
- 5 = "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others"
- 4 = "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others"
- 3 = "Interesting: I might mention some points of this paper to others"
- 2 = "Potentially Interesting: this paper does not resonate with me"
- 1 = "Not Exciting: this paper does not resonate with me"

**Reviewer Confidence** (1-5 scale)
- 5 = Positive evaluation is correct; familiar with related work
- 4 = Quite sure; unlikely to have missed important points
- 3 = Pretty sure; didn't check all details carefully
- 2 = Willing to defend; fairly likely missed important details
- 1 = Not my area or paper is very difficult to understand

**Overall Assessment** (1-5 scale with half-point increments)
- 5 = Consider for Award (top 2.5%)
- 4 = Conference acceptance
- 3 = Findings acceptance
- 2 = Resubmit next cycle
- 1 = Do not resubmit

**Reproducibility** (1-5 scale)
- 5 = Results could be easily reproduced
- 4 = Results mostly reproducible with minor variations
- 3 = Results reproducible with difficulty
- 2 = Results would be hard to reproduce
- 1 = Results cannot be reproduced

**Datasets & Software** (1-5 scales, separately)
- 5 = Enabling resource affecting research direction
- 4 = Useful for ongoing work
- 3 = Potentially useful
- 2 = Documentary value only
- 1 = No usable resources submitted

**Author Identity Knowledge** (1-5 scale)

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查新颖性、相关工作完整性、写作质量和格式合规。

## Venue 信息
- **目标会议**: EMNLP 2026 (ACL Rolling Review May 2026 cycle)
- **投稿类别**: Long Paper (ARR)
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25
- **页数限制**: 正文 8 页，参考文献无限，Limitations/Ethics 不计入页数
- **模板**: ACL (使用 \usepackage[review]{acl})
- **匿名规则**: Double-blind（无匿名期要求）
- **评分标准**: ARR Overall Assessment 1-5 分制（5=Award, 4=Conference, 3=Findings, 2=Resubmit, 1=Do not resubmit）

## 论文信息
- **标题**: The Capability Ceiling of Self-Consistency: How Entity-Level Self-Verification Fails in Named Entity Recognition
- **论文文件**: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.tex`
- **PDF**: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.pdf`

## 你的审查侧重

### 1. 新颖性评估
- "Correlation-selection gap" 概念的新颖性：是否为已知现象的重新包装？与 MBR decoding 文献的区别？
- Diagnostic cascade（QE → degeneracy → agreement fidelity → LP compression）作为分析框架的贡献度
- 9 种方法的覆盖度：是否遗漏了重要的方法类别（如 Self-Certainty, P(True), 其他 per-sample scoring methods）？
- Hidden-state probe 的 "introspection boundary" 概念是否有独立贡献？
- Entity construction 作为 prescriptive contribution 的新颖性（vs. 已有 entity voting 工作如 Xie et al. 2023）
- Cross-task validation（RE + SQL）对论文 scope claims 的支撑力度

### 2. 相关工作完整性
- Test-time compute scaling 文献覆盖度
- SC failure modes（Chen et al. 2023, Huang et al. 2024）引用和差异化是否充分
- MBR decoding 文献（Eikema & Aziz 2022, DeNero et al. 2009 等）
- NER-specific SC 工作（Yao et al. 2026, Xie et al. 2023）
- Diversity collapse（Yun et al. 2025）差异化是否充分
- Uncertainty estimation / calibration 文献
- 是否有遗漏的关键引用（特别是 2025-2026 年的新工作）
- Bib 文件质量：是否有 orphan entries、虚构引用、元数据错误？

### 3. 写作质量
- Abstract 信息密度是否适当？是否清晰传达了 4 个 contributions？
- Introduction 的叙事结构：问题定义 → 方法 → 结果 → 贡献是否连贯？
- 各 section 之间的逻辑流是否顺畅？
- 术语一致性（如 F1 metric 的定义和使用、degeneracy 的定义）
- 因果语言 vs 相关性语言的使用是否与证据强度匹配
- Table/Figure 的设计和 caption 质量
- Limitations section 是否充分覆盖主要局限

### 4. 格式合规
- 正文是否在 8 页限制内？
- 匿名化是否完整（无作者信息泄露、无自引暴露身份）？
- 引用格式是否符合 ACL 标准？
- Responsible NLP Research Checklist 是否完整？
- 附录引用是否正确？

### 5. 审稿报告格式
请按以下格式提交审稿意见：
- **Summary**: 2-3 句论文概述
- **Strengths**: 逐条列出优点
- **Weaknesses**: 逐条列出缺点（标注 [critical] / [should-fix] / [minor]）
- **Questions**: 需要作者回答的问题
- **Missing References**: 遗漏的重要相关工作
- **Overall Assessment**: 基于 ARR 评分标准的初步评分 + 置信度
- **Detailed Comments**: 逐节评论

_source line 2813 · 2026-05-21T13:06:10.225324+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	Test-time compute scaling through repeated sampling yields large gains on discrete-answer reasoning and code generation, yet for named entity recognition (NER), where outputs are entity sets with partial overlap, a persistent gap separates quality estimation from sample selection.
3	We ask whether entity-level agreement signals can bridge this gap.
4	Across nine method categories spanning diagnostic probes, selection/construction strategies, and learning-based approaches, we find that no entity-level agreement signal reliably improves selection.
5	Quality estimation signals correlate with oracle F1 ($\rho$ up to 0.47, AUROC up to 0.82) yet no signal selects better than greedy decoding.
6	The failure is systematic: a four-stage diagnostic cascade (quality estimation $\to$ degeneracy $\to$ agreement fidelity $\to$ log-probability compression) localizes where and how selection breaks down, with cross-task validation on RE and SQL parsing (87.2\% output degeneracy).
7	A hidden-state linear probe achieves entity-level discrimination (area under the ROC curve [AUROC] $0.63$\textendash$0.88$, dataset-dependent) from a single forward pass, consistently exceeding prompt-based self-evaluation (AUROC 0.51) but matching multi-sample agreement ($0.87$) only on the simplest task.
8	These negative results delineate where self-consistency (SC) does succeed for IE: through paths that bypass entity-level agreement.
9	Log-probability selection improves F1 by $+1.39$\,pp on Few-NERD (Holm-adjusted $p{=}0.021$, selection family $k{=}21$), entity-level construction via weighted voting gains $+1.02$\textendash$1.40$\,pp, and cross-model ensembles add $+0.42$\,pp on Few-NERD.
10	Each exploits sequence-level or cross-model signals rather than entity-level self-verification.
11	A degeneracy decomposition confirms SC gain vanishes once output degeneracy exceeds 70\%.
12	\end{abstract}
13	%% REFRAME: scope precision + empirical exhaustion methodology
14

1	\section{Introduction}\label{sec:introduction}
2	
3	Test-time compute scaling complements model scaling \cite{snell2025scaling}: repeated sampling with majority voting \cite{wang2023selfconsistency}, best-of-$N$ with learned verifiers \cite{cobbe2021verifiers, brown2024monkeys}, and extended chain-of-thought \cite{muennighoff2025s1} produce large gains on reasoning and code generation.
4	These successes share a structural prerequisite: the output space admits either exact equivalence classes (multiple-choice, numeric answers) or execution-based verification (code).
5	Named entity recognition (NER) breaks both assumptions.
6	The output is a set of typed entities; two samples from the same input routinely extract overlapping but non-identical sets, and no external oracle can adjudicate correctness at test time.
7	Without equivalence classes, majority voting reduces to heuristic frequency counting; without a verifier, best-of-$N$ requires a reliable proxy signal.
8	This structural mismatch raises a concrete question: can self-consistency signals that estimate quality well for reasoning tasks also \textit{select} better outputs or \textit{construct} better entity sets for NER?
9	We investigate this question for NER and validate the core findings on SQL parsing, treating NER as a representative instance of the broader set-valued generation problem.
10	
11	The question has practical stakes beyond information extraction.
12	Hallucination detection methods such as SelfCheckGPT \cite{manakul2023selfcheckgpt} and FActScore \cite{min2023factscore} rely on cross-sample consistency to verify individual claims: if a model's outputs agree, the shared content is presumed factual.
13	This reasoning assumes entity-level self-verification works, that a model can reliably distinguish correct extractions from incorrect ones through self-judgment or re-prompting.
14	We show this assumption fails.
15	Three independent probes (entity self-judgment, multi-round re-prompting, latent-space

1	\section{Diagnostic Framework}\label{sec:method}
2	
3	\subsection{Problem Formulation}\label{sec:formulation}
4	
5	Given model $\mathcal{M}$ and input $x$, we sample $N$ outputs $\{y_1, \ldots, y_N\}$ at temperature $T > 0$ via constrained decoding \cite{willard2023guided}, where each $y_i$ is an entity set.
6	A \textbf{per-sample signal} $c_{\text{ps}}: (x, y_i) \to \mathbb{R}$ scores each output independently; an \textbf{instance-level signal} $c_{\text{il}}: (x, \{y_i\}_{i=1}^N) \to \mathbb{R}$ produces a single score per input (notation in Appendix~\ref{app:notation}).
7	This distinction is consequential: 4 of 5 signals are instance-level aggregates assigning identical scores to all $N$ candidates.
8	Only LP carries per-sample discriminative power: ensemble optimization assigns LP mean weight $0.78$, with structural signals receiving zero weight (Appendix~\ref{app:supervised_combination}).
9	When LP fails---as in few-shot regimes where LP $\rho{\to}0$ (\S\ref{sec:scaling})---no alternative per-sample signal exists.
10	
11	\subsection{Confidence Signals}\label{sec:signals}
12	
13	Entity matching uses exact span and type match.
14	We evaluate four \textit{consistency signals}---\textit{structural} (Soft Jaccard [SJ], entity voting confidence [VC]) and \textit{surface} (exact match [EM], Fleiss' $\kappa$ [FK])---and log-probability [LP] as the sole \textit{model-internal}, per-sample signal.

16	\paragraph{Log-probability (per-sample).}
17	$c_{\text{lp}}(y_i) = |y_i|^{-1} \sum_{j} \log p_{\mathcal{M}}(t_j \mid t_{<j}, x)$.

19	\paragraph{Soft Jaccard (structural, instance-level).}
20	For samples $y_i, y_j$, we group by type, compute max-weight bipartite matching (Hungarian algorithm, token-level IoU):

22	\text{SJ}(y_i, y_j) = \frac{\sum_{m \in M} \text{sim}(a_m, b_m)}{\sum_{g} \max(|g_i|, |g_j|)}
23	\end{equation}

1	\section{How Self-Verification Fails}\label{sec:mechanistic}
2	
3	The diagnostic cascade (\S\ref{sec:method}) identifies \textit{where} self-consistency fails; this section probes \textit{why} by testing whether models can self-verify at the entity level.
4	We evaluate self-verification and diagnostic probes on Qwen2.5-7B (a separate model family from the primary Qwen3-8B) fine-tuned for NER on Few-NERD ($N{=}8$, seed 42; full results in Appendix~\ref{app:self_verification}).
5	
6	\paragraph{Entity Self-Judgment (ESJ).}
7	For each extracted entity, we prompt the generating model: ``Is [\textit{entity}] a [\textit{type}] entity in this sentence?''
8	Entity-level AUROC is $0.512$---indistinguishable from chance---with P(yes$|$correct)${=}0.500$ and P(yes$|$incorrect)${=}0.500$.
9	ESJ-weighted construction degrades F1 to $0.711$ (vs.\ greedy $0.794$).
10	
11	\paragraph{Multi-Round Self-Consistency (MRSC).}
12	Re-prompting on disputed entities (agreement ${<}0.6$) with focused verification yields P(yes) separation of $0.0006$ between gold and non-gold entities ($0.5003$ vs.\ $0.4997$).
13	MRSC best F1 ($0.763$) falls below greedy.
14	
15	\paragraph{Latent Self-Consistency (LSC).}
16	Mean-pooled final-layer hidden states ($d{=}3584$) provide per-sample latent representations.
17	Centroid selection ($0.759$) underperforms greedy ($0.794$); latent entity confidence ($0.839$) outperforms majority vote ($0.792$) but does not close the oracle gap ($0.882$).
18	
19	\paragraph{Hidden-state probe.}
20	A logistic regression on mean-pooled final-layer hidden states at entity spans achieves AUROC $0.636$ (Few-NERD), $0.882$ (CoNLL), $0.630$ (SciERC), evaluated with GroupKFold CV (5-fold ${\times}$ 3 seeds, groups${=}$source instance to prevent entity-level leakage; Appendix~\ref{app:hidden_probe}).

1	\section{Where Single-Signal Selection Fails}\label{sec:selection_gap}
2	
3	\paragraph{Definitional vs.\ empirical.}
4	We decompose the correlation-selection gap into two layers.
5	The first is structural: four of five signals are instance-level aggregates that assign identical scores to all $N$ candidates from a given input (\S\ref{sec:formulation}), so they estimate quality across inputs but cannot rank candidates within an instance---selection failure is a design property, not an empirical finding.
6	The second layer is empirical and constitutes the core diagnostic: LP---the only per-sample signal---suffers compression under constrained decoding, with 60\% of SciERC instances showing within-instance LP range below 0.041 nats (Appendix~\ref{app:logprob_ties}), effectively eliminating the variation needed for sample discrimination. Even where LP varies, selection gains depend on LP-quality alignment rather than degeneracy alone.
7	Cross-instance $\rho$ (Table~\ref{tab:quality_estimation}) is attenuated by varying instance difficulty; within-instance $\rho$ directly measures whether LP discriminates candidates for a fixed input, making it the relevant predictor of selection success.

1	\section{Threshold Regime Analysis}\label{sec:theory_scaling}
2	
3	\subsection{Entity Construction as MBR Decoding}\label{sec:mbr_theory}
4	
5	Following the MBR framework (Appendix~\ref{app:mbr_proof}), entity-level construction selects $\hat{y}_{\textup{MBR}} = \{e \in E_\cup : \hat{p}(e) > \alpha/(\alpha{+}1)\}$ under balanced set-overlap utility; $\alpha{=}1$ recovers the majority-vote threshold $\hat{p}(e) > \tfrac{1}{2}$ used throughout (Proposition~\ref{prop:construction_mbr}; empirical validation: Table~\ref{tab:entity_construction}).
6	Thus majority vote is MBR decoding under set-overlap utility.
7	Signal-derived weights $w_i$ yield quality-weighted MBR (LP-weighted construction): $\hat{p}_w(e) = \sum_{i} w_i \, \mathbf{1}[e {\in} y_i] / \sum_{i} w_i$.
8	Weighted MBR improves when $w_i$ correlates with $u(y_i, y^*)$ within instances; when this correlation is weak (LP on SciERC: $\rho{\approx}0.2$; few-shot: \S\ref{sec:scaling}), weighting degrades (\S\ref{sec:prescriptive}).
9	
10	\subsection{Scaling and Inverse Scaling}\label{sec:scaling}
11	
12	Oracle F1 follows $F_1(N) = a - b \cdot N^{-c}$ ($R^2 > 0.999$; empirical fit in Figure~\ref{fig:oracle_scaling}): Few-NERD ($c{\approx}0.61$) scales comparably to reasoning tasks \cite{brown2024monkeys}, while SciERC converges more slowly ($c{\approx}0.35$).
13	The ceiling grows, but confidence-based aggregation recovers ${<}1$\,pp---a persistent aggregation gap.
14	The gap persists across scales and families: scaling from 8B to 14B FT increases oracle headroom ($+1.2$\,pp) with minimal greedy gain ($+0.9$\,pp), and GPT-5.5 (200 Few-NERD instances, zero-shot) exhibits the same pattern---50\% degeneracy, SJ $\rho{=}0.346$, MV $+1.4$\,pp yet 6.8\,pp oracle headroom unrealized.\footnote{10.1\% of GPT-5.5 samples produced unparseable output; these are excluded from all metrics. See Appendix~\ref{app:frontier_sc}.}

…[truncated 3131 chars]

1	\section{Experiments}\label{sec:experiments}
2	
3	\subsection{Experimental Setup}\label{sec:setup}
4	
5	\paragraph{Datasets.}
6	We evaluate on four English NER benchmarks spanning a difficulty gradient.
7	\textbf{SciERC} \cite{luan2018scierc} (551 test instances, 6 entity types) is the primary medium-difficulty test bed (${\approx}$65\% F1).
8	\textbf{Few-NERD} \cite{ding2021fewnerd} (INTRA coarse; 37{,}648 instances, 8 entity types, ${\approx}$75\% F1) provides a low-degeneracy contrast where LP selection succeeds (\S\ref{sec:selection_gap}).
9	\textbf{CoNLL-2003} \cite{tjongkimsang2003conll} (3{,}453 test sentences, 4 entity types, ${\approx}$91\% F1) serves as a ceiling-effect control.
10	\textbf{WNUT-17} \cite{derczynski2017wnut} (1{,}287 social media instances, 51\% zero F1) probes the signal-collapse boundary.
11	
12	\paragraph{Models and sampling.}
13	\textbf{Qwen3-8B} and \textbf{LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct} are LoRA fine-tuned using LLaMA Factory \cite{zheng2024llamafactory} (Appendix~\ref{app:training}; Few-NERD uses 3 epochs vs.\ 5 for others; Appendix~\ref{app:epoch_confound}).
14	$N{=}8$ samples at $T{=}1.0$ (primary) with vLLM constrained decoding \cite{willard2023guided}; $N{=}16$ for scaling (Appendix~\ref{app:n_scaling}).

1	\section{Related Work}\label{sec:related}
2	
3	\paragraph{Test-time compute scaling.}
4	Self-consistency \cite{wang2023selfconsistency} and best-of-$N$ \cite{snell2025scaling, brown2024monkeys} drive large gains on reasoning and code.
5	Reward-free selectors \cite{taubenfeld2025cisc, kang2025selfcertainty}, BoN scaling laws \cite{gao2023scaling}, adaptive allocation \cite{aggarwal2023adaptive}, and trained verifiers \cite{cobbe2021verifiers, lightman2024verify} all target discrete-answer settings; structured prediction requires different aggregation because degeneracy operates at the entity-set level.
6	
7	\paragraph{SC failure modes and fine-grained variants.}
8	\citet{chen2023twofailures} identify SC failures where consistent outputs are systematically wrong; \citet{huang2024large} show LLMs cannot self-correct reasoning without external feedback. Our correlation-selection gap is a distinct failure where signals \textit{correlate} with quality yet fail to \textit{select}.
9	\citet{chen2024usc} extend SC to free-form tasks; our diagnostic shows such extensions face limits under constrained decoding (\S\ref{sec:selection_gap}). \citet{thirukovalluru2024atomic} decompose claims atomically; our entity-level decomposition reveals sub-output agreement does not translate to selection gain.
10	
11	\paragraph{Consistency for structured IE.}
12	\citet{yao2026consistency} show consistency correlates with NER/RE accuracy; we diagnose degeneracy and LP compression as mechanisms that prevent this correlation from yielding selection gains.
13	\citet{xie2023zeroshot} propose entity voting for zero-shot NER and \citet{li2024simple} improve structured output formatting; our approach diagnoses why entity-level agreement fails to improve selection, building on generative IE \cite{zhou2024uniner, sainz2024gollie} and constrained decoding \cite{willard2023guided, dong2024xgrammar}.
14	
15	\paragraph{Uncertainty estimation and MBR.}
16	Our selection instantiates MBR decoding \cite{eik
…[truncated 1152 chars]

1	\section{Discussion}\label{sec:discussion}
2	
3	NER exhibits oracle scaling ($c{\approx}0.5$; \S\ref{sec:scaling}), but degeneracy (17.8--67.9\%) and LP-quality alignment ($\rho$ 0.043--0.667) constrain aggregation; temperature disentangles these at $T{=}1.2$ (Table~\ref{tab:t_degeneracy}).
4	
5	\paragraph{Degeneracy correlates with the selection gap across model families.}
6	Across four frontier models on Few-NERD, degeneracy and the selection gap (oracle${-}$MV) are negatively correlated (5.5--74\% degen $\to$ 0.290--0.032 gap; Table~\ref{tab:frontier_sc_cross}), consistent with the degeneracy bound (\S\ref{sec:theory}) being structural rather than model-specific.
7	
8	\paragraph{From diagnosis to practice.}\label{sec:selection_method}
9	Entity-level construction bypasses both failure mechanisms at zero marginal cost, with gains positive when degeneracy ${<}22$\% and absent when ${>}31$\% ($p{<}0.01$; Appendix~\ref{app:epoch_confound}); alternative strategies fail to beat greedy (Appendix~\ref{app:dals}, \ref{app:supervised_combination}).
10	Construction is the only consistently positive strategy (Table~\ref{tab:cost_benefit}), though the trade-off ($N{\times}$ inference cost for ${\approx}1$\,pp) may only be justified in safety-critical settings.
11	LP compression (median 0.041 nats) connects to calibration challenges \cite{kadavath2022language, xiong2024verbalized}; per-sample alternatives \cite{kang2025selfcertainty} remain untested.
12	
13	\paragraph{Limitations of causal interpretation.}
14	Our evidence is correlational and theoretical (MBR framework, conditional decomposition), not interventional: we have not performed controlled interventions (e.g., artificially varying degeneracy while holding other factors constant), and the cascade stages interact in ways that resist decomposition into independent causal effects ($R^2{<}0$; \S\ref{sec:selection_gap}).
15	Our contribution is characterization of the phenomenon; causal claims require future intervention e
…[truncated 15 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}\label{sec:conclusion}
2	
3	NER exhibits oracle scaling ($c{\approx}0.5$) but aggregation fails above $\theta_{\text{crit}}{=}0.70$; entity construction and LP selection ($+0.4$\textendash$1.4$\,pp) bypass the introspection boundary (AUROC $0.63$\textendash$0.88$ vs.\ ESJ $0.51$) rather than resolve it.
4	For configurations with small sample sizes (e.g., SQL $n{=}196$), absence of significant improvement may reflect insufficient statistical power rather than true absence of effect.
5

1	Scope: structured IE (NER primary, RE and SQL exploratory), English only, open-weight (Qwen and LLaMA) and closed-source (GPT-5.5) families, fine-tuned (4B--14B) and zero-shot (72B, GPT-5.5) regimes, sample budget $N{\leq}32$.
2	Our evaluation covers four NER datasets; an exploratory RE extension on SciERC is reported in Appendix~\ref{app:re_extension} but generalization to other RE benchmarks remains unvalidated.
3	All experiments use English-language datasets; cross-lingual generalization remains untested.
4	Cross-model consistency results (\S\ref{sec:cross_model}) are limited to one dataset (Few-NERD) and one model pair (Qwen3-8B + LLaMA-3.1-8B).
5	Entity-level construction assumes the model produces useful partial outputs across samples; when model capability is insufficient (WNUT-17, 51\% F1${=}0$), this precondition fails and construction cannot improve over greedy.
6	We cover 4B\textendash 14B fine-tuned, 72B zero-shot, and GPT-5.5 zero-shot; the gap persists across all but behavior with 72B fine-tuning is untested.
7	Frontier API models exhibit markedly different degeneracy profiles (e.g., Claude Sonnet~4 at 74\% vs.\ DeepSeek Chat at 5.5\%; Table~\ref{tab:frontier_sc_cross}), suggesting that our findings from fine-tuned open-weight models may not fully generalize to proprietary systems.
8	The 72B zero-shot comparison simultaneously varies model family (Qwen3$\to$Qwen2.5), training regime (fine-tuned$\to$zero-shot), and quantization (FP16$\to$AWQ 4-bit); these factors cannot be fully disentangled.
9	Our supervised approaches (DeBERTa verifier, hidden state probes, MLP/RF on signal combinations) all fail to close the oracle gap; whether content-aware verifiers with access to task-specific structure could succeed remains open.
10	
11	We evaluate at $N{=}8$ (primary) and $N{=}16$; behavior at larger sample sizes is unknown (Appendix~\ref{app:n_scaling}).
12	The free-form ablation covers SciERC NER and CoNLL NER with one model; RE free-form results are in Appe

29	\section{Robustness Analysis}\label{app:robustness}
30	
31	We exclude instances with empty gold annotations from all correlation analyses (CoNLL: 697/3{,}453 instances, 20.2\%; SciERC: 22/551, 4.0\%).
32	For these instances, F1 is trivially zero regardless of prediction quality, and consistency signals reflect agreement on the empty set rather than extraction quality. Including them inflates or deflates Spearman $\rho$ depending on signal behavior at the boundary.

1007	\section{Theoretical Results}\label{app:mbr_discussion}
1008	
1009	\subsection{MBR Framework and Proof}\label{app:mbr_proof}
1010	
1011	Entity-level construction (\S\ref{sec:prescriptive}) is a special case of minimum Bayes risk (MBR) decoding \cite{kumar2004minimum, eikema2020map}, extended from candidate selection ($\arg\max_{y \in \{y_1,\ldots,y_N\}}$) to construction over all subsets of $E_\cup{=}\bigcup_i y_i$.
1012	
1013	\begin{proposition}[Entity construction as MBR for balanced set-overlap]\label{prop:construction_mbr}
1014	Under entity-wise independence with utility $u(\hat{y}, y') = |\hat{y} \cap y'| - \alpha|\hat{y} \setminus y'|$ ($\alpha \geq 1$), the MBR-optimal constructed set is
1015	\begin{equation}\label{eq:mbr_construction}
1016	\hat{y}_{\textup{MBR}} \!=\! \bigl\{e \!{\in}\! E_\cup \!:\! \hat{p}(e) \!>\! \tfrac{\alpha}{\alpha{+}1}\bigr\},\; \hat{p}(e) \!=\! \tfrac{1}{N} \textstyle\sum\nolimits_{i} \mathbf{1}[e \!{\in}\! y_i]
1017	\end{equation}
1018	The special case $\alpha{=}1$ yields $\hat{p}(e) > \tfrac{1}{2}$, the majority-vote threshold.
1019	\end{proposition}
1020	
1021	\noindent\textbf{Remark.}
1022	Set-level F1 is non-linear and admits no closed-form threshold rule \cite{dembczynski2011exact}. We use balanced linear utility ($\alpha{=}1$ throughout); balanced set-overlap and F1 induce similar optimal thresholds for typical NER output sizes ($|E_\cup| \leq 30$).

## Test-Time Compute Scaling

Parallel scaling strategies improve performance by aggregating answers across multiple independently sampled reasoning traces, with self-consistency sampling diverse reasoning paths to choose the most frequent final answer. Several efforts have attempted to replicate the performance of o1 models by training LLMs to scale up test-time compute, with DeepSeek-R1 demonstrating that LLMs can naturally increase test-time compute and show consistent performance improvements through a pure reinforcement learning process.

GENCLUSTER is a scalable test-time compute framework that combines large-scale generation, behavioral clustering, ranking, and a round-robin submission strategy to efficiently explore diverse solution spaces under limited validation budgets.

Based on the search results, I found relevant information about MBR decoding and structured prediction. Here are the key findings:

## MBR Decoding in Structured Prediction and NER

While MBR has proven effective in machine translation, where the variability of a language model's outcome space is naturally constrained, it may face challenges in more open-ended tasks such as dialogue or instruction-following.

MBR casts decoding as a decision-theoretic problem, where the selected sequence is the one that minimises risk with respect to a task-specific utility function, under the uncertainty over continuations represented by the language model. This utility typically reflects the degree of agreement between a candidate and the broader set of outcomes, penalising candidates that diverge significantly from the consensus.

I found relevant results for your search. Here's what I found:

The paper "Uncertainty-Aware Decoding with Minimum Bayes Risk" was published at ICLR 2025.

The work shows how Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding can be generalized into a principled uncertainty-aware decoding method by incorporating a posterior over model parameters into MBR's computation of expected risk.

The research addresses the problem that language models occasionally generate undesirable outputs like hallucinated text, and notes there is a lack of methods that actively consider uncertainty during text generation.

The modified expected risk is useful for both choosing outputs and deciding when to abstain from generation and can provide improvements without incurring overhead.

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查新颖性和完整性。

## 论文信息
- 标题: "The Capability Ceiling of Self-Consistency: How Entity-Level Self-Verification Fails in Named Entity Recognition"
- 目标 venue: EMNLP 2026（通过 ARR 统一审稿）
- 投稿类别: Main Conference, Long Paper
- Deadline: 2026-05-25
- 论文路径: projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.tex
- PDF 路径: /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.pdf

## Venue 格式要求
- 页数限制: 正文 8 页（不含 references），Limitations 和 Ethics 不计入页数（但不得包含过多额外内容）
- 模板: ACL 2-column (acl.sty, review mode)
- 匿名规则: Double-blind
- 引用格式: natbib
- 附录: 允许，但不保证审稿人会阅读
- 评分标准: 5=Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1.5=Resubmit after next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重

**新颖性 + 完整性**，重点关注：

1. **贡献定位**：4 个 contribution 是否真正新颖？"correlation-selection gap" 概念在 MBR/self-consistency 文献中是否已有类似发现？
2. **Related Work 覆盖**：是否遗漏了 key references？特别是：
   - MBR decoding for structured prediction
   - Self-verification / self-evaluation in LLMs（SelfCheckGPT, FActScore 之外）
   - Entity-level confidence estimation
   - Test-time compute scaling 最新工作
   - Structured output generation with LLMs
3. **叙事逻辑**：论文从 "SC fails for NER" 到 "prescriptive escape routes" 的叙事弧是否流畅？Introduction 的 claim 是否被后续实验充分支持？
4. **负面结果论文的价值**：对 EMNLP community 的启发性如何？是否仅仅是 "X doesn't work" 还是提供了深层理解？
5. **格式合规**：
   - 正文是否超过 8 页限制？
   - Double-blind 匿名是否完整（无作者信息泄露、无 GitHub 链接、无 "our previous work" 等）？
   - Limitations section 是否存在且内容充分？
   - References 格式是否一致（会议名 vs 缩写、年份、页码）？
6. **写作质量**：术语一致性、图表标注清晰度、符号使用规范性
7. **Missing references**：列出你认为应该引用但未引用的重要工作

请提供完整的结构化审稿报告：Summary → Strengths → Weaknesses → Questions → Missing References → Minor Issues。每个 Weakness 标注严重程度（Must-Fix / Should-Fix / Nice-to-Have）。

Based on the search results, here's what I found about ACL ARR 2025/2026 reviewer guidelines and scoring:

## Reviewer Scoring System

Starting with February 2025, ARR reviewers are asked to provide three scores: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation.

## Score Justification Requirements

The soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review. If you give a low Soundness score without finding any major faults, this means that your review is not a faithful explanation of your recommendation, and you need to revise it.

## Soundness vs. Excitement

Sometimes you may find the work sound, but not something that is interesting for you personally. Such preferences are more subjective and should be reflected in a separate excitement score, which is orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness of the paper.

## Area Chair Meta-review Scoring

The ARR metareview score has changed in February 2025 to be more aligned with reviewer scores. Like with reviews, the score should be backed up by the meta-review text. The ne

_source line 2892 · 2026-05-21T15:04:06.385357+00:00_

# ARR Reviewer Scoring Criteria

## Scoring Dimensions

The document specifies **three primary scores** that reviewers must provide:

1. **Soundness** - "must be justified by the text of the review." Low soundness scores require explicit flaws identified in the paper; unsupported low scores suggest unconscious bias.

2. **Excitement** - "orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness of the paper." This reflects subjective personal interest and "do not necessarily come with explicit reasons."

3. **Overall Assessment** - "an explicit recommendation for the outcome" if committed to an ACL venue. This is a "composite score reflecting your assessment of soundness, excitement, and also other factors like novelty and impact."

## Score Range & Guidance

The document does not specify exact numerical ranges for these dimensions. However, it establishes key principles:

- All papers recommended for publication "are expected to be sufficiently sound and reproducible"
- Reviewers may find work sound yet personally uninterested (reflected in separate excitement scoring)
- Papers can merit acceptance even without high excitement if they demonstrate significant impact potential

## Additional Evaluation Focus Areas

Reviewers should assess:
- **Reproducibility** - sufficient details, code/data availability, hyperparameter transparency
- **Methodological rigor** - appropriate statistical analysis, complete proofs, clear assumptions
- **Ethical considerations** - data collection practices, potential harms to marginalized groups

The guidelines emphasize that "soundness scores without sufficient justification" represent review failures requiring revision.

## Self-Refine: Iterative Refinement with Self-Feedback

Self-Refine is an approach by Aman Madaan and colleagues for improving initial outputs from LLMs through iterative feedback and refinement, presented at NeurIPS 2023.

**Core Concept:**
The main idea is to generate an initial output using an LLM; then, the same LLM provides feedback for its output and uses it to refine itself, iteratively.

**Key Features:**
- Self-Refine does not require any super

I found several recent papers and resources on test-time compute scaling and structured prediction from 2025-2026. Here are the key findings:

## Test-Time Scaling Overview

Test-time scaling (TTS) refers to the strategy of trading more computational resources for more predictive accuracy at inference time. By trading additional inference-time compute for accuracy, test-time scaling improves the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) while keeping model parameters unchanged.

## Key Research Areas

**Scaling Laws and Compute Allocation:** Recent studies empirically show that allocating more inference compute to smaller models via repeated sampling can match or exceed the performance of larger ones. No existing work jointly optimizes model size, training duration, and the number of inference samples under a single compute budget.

**Structured Prediction Approaches:** GenCluster, a scalable test-time compute approach, consists of four stages: (1) parallel generation, (2) behavioral clustering, (3) ranking with tournament,

```json
{
  "text": "## Reviewer 2 审稿报告：新颖性 + 完整性\n\n**论文**: \"The Capability Ceiling of Self-Consistency: How Entity-Level Self-Verification Fails in Named Entity Recognition\"\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR)\n**类别**: Main Conference, Long Paper\n\n---\n\n## 总体评分\n\n**Overall**: 3.5 — Borderline Conference\n**一句话总结**: 一篇执行扎实的负面结果诊断研究，提出了有用的\"correlation-selection gap\"概念框架，但核心发现的新颖性有限（SC 对结构化预测效果差在 MBR 文献中已有先兆），且关键统计结果在严格多重比较校正下未能存活。\n\n---\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 方法论整体严谨（Holm-Bonferroni 校正、bootstrap CI、TOST 等价检验），但核心 claim 在全局校正下 p_adj=0.055 未达显著；SciERC 仅 n=529 且 seed 敏感性 σ=0.48 pp；部分 escape route 增益在噪声范围内 |\n| Excitement | 3/5 | 诊断框架（4-stage cascade）是有用的概念贡献，但\"SC 对结构化输出效果差\"对熟悉 MBR 文献的读者并不意外；escape routes 增益微弱（+1.0–1.4 pp）；负面结果价值中等 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 实验细节充分，checklist 完整，承诺代码开源；超参数、训练配置、评估协议均有记录 |\n| Overall Assessment | 3.5/5 | Borderline Conference — 执行质量高但新颖性中等，更适合 Findings |\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] 核心统计 claim 的稳健性不足\n- 具体位置：Section 7.5 (Table 5)、Appendix f (Table 13)\n- 问题：论文承认在全局 Holm-Bonferroni 校正（k=57）下，\"no core NER claim survives\"（p_adj=0.055 for Few-NERD LP selection and construction）。论文转而报告 per-family 校正（selection k=21, construction k=10），但 per-family 分割本身是事后决定，削弱了统计严谨性。Few-NERD LP selection 的 per-family p_adj=0.021 是唯一正面结果，而 SciERC construction 的 p_adj=0.20 不显著。\n- 为什么影响接收：论文 4 个 contribution 中的第 4 个（\"prescriptive escape routes\"）依赖这些统计结果。如果核心正面结果在合理的多重比较校正下不显著，那么\"escape routes\"的贡献就被大幅削弱，论文变成纯负面结果 + 诊断框架。\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 在正文中明确讨论全局 vs per-family 校正的取舍，而非仅在 Appendix f 披露；(2) 预注册 hypothesis families 或提供更强的 justification；(3) 考虑用 permutation test 或 Bayesian 方法补充频率主义检验。\n\n**N2** [critical] \"Correlation-selection gap\" 概念的新颖性定位不够精准\n- 具体位置：Section 1 (lines 94–95)、Section 4、Section 8\n- 问题：论文将 \"correlation-selection gap\" 作为核心概念贡献，但未充分区分与 MBR 文献中已知发现的关系。Eikema & Aziz (2020, 2022) 已经证明 MAP decoding 的不足，Eikema et al. (2025) 明确指出 MBR utilities 在结构化输出上表现退化。论文的 Related Work (Section 8, lines 533–548) 讨论了 MBR 但主要聚焦于 utility function 选择，未明确论证 \"correlation-selection gap\" 相比 \"MBR utility degrade on structured outputs\" 的增量贡献。Instance-level signal 的设计性局限（4/5 信号是 instance-level 因此无法区分 per-sample 候选，Section 2.1 lines 162–173）更像是方法设计问题而非深层发现。\n- 为什么影响接收：新颖性是论文能否从 Findings 升级到 Main Conference 的关键判据。如果核心概念在先行工作中已有类似表述，贡献定位就需要更精确。\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 在 Related Work 中增加一段 explicit comparison，说明 MBR utility degradation (Eikema et al., 2025) 与 correlation-selection gap 的区别和联系；(2) 强调诊断 cascade 的增量价值——MBR 文献指出问题存在，本文定位问题在哪个环节发生（degeneracy vs. LP compression），这是真正的新贡献。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] 模型覆盖范围有限，泛化性声明过度\n- 具体位置：Section 7.1 (lines 425–431)、Limitations (lines 600–618)\n- 问题：完整实验仅覆盖 Qwen3-8B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B（均为 LoRA 微调）。72B zero-shot 扩展仅在 Few-NERD 上进行，GPT-5.5 仅 200 实例。论文在 Limitations 中承认 frontier API models（Claude Sonnet 4, DeepSeek Chat）表现出不同的 degeneracy profiles（4% vs 5.5%），\"findings from fine-tuned open-weight models may not fully generalize to proprietary systems\"（lines 619–624）。但 Introduction 和 Conclusion 的措辞（如 \"persistent gap\"、\"capability ceiling\"）暗示普遍性结论。\n- 建议：弱化泛化性措辞，明确区分 \"fine-tuned open-weight models under constrained decoding\" 和一般性结论；或增加至少一个 proprietary model 的完整实验。\n\n**W2** [warning] Cross-task validation 较薄弱\n- 具体位置：Section 1 (lines 125–126)、Appendix o\n- 问题：RE 验证仅在 SciERC 一个数据集上进行，且 RE F1 被 entity span errors 严重干扰（55.5% 的 zero-F1 RE instances 来自 span 错误，footnote 1）。SQL parsing 仅提到 87.2% degeneracy 但缺乏系统分析。论文声称 NER 是 \"broader set-valued generation problem\" 的代表（line 68），但跨任务证据不够支撑这一定位。\n- 建议：(1) 如果 RE 数据受 span 错误污染严重，应讨论这对 cross-task validation 的影响；(2) SQL parsing 需更详细的分析或降低 cross-task generalization 的 claim。\n\n**W3** [warning] 遗漏直接相关的参考文献\n- 具体位置：Section 8 (Related Work)\n- 问题：以下工作与本文高度相关但未被引用：\n  1. **Ma et al. (2024) \"Self-Improving for Zero-Shot Named Entity Recognition with Large Language Models\" (NAACL 2024)** — 直接使用 self-consistency 进行 NER annotation selection，是对 SC 在 NER 上应用的直接先行工作，论文应讨论其发现与本文的关系\n  2. **Madaan et al. (2023) \"Self-Refine: Iterative Refinement with Self-Feedback\" (NeurIPS 2023)** — 论文测试了 Multi-Round Self-Consistency (MRSC, Section 3)，但未引用最相关的 iterative self-refinement 基准工作\n- 为什么重要：Ma et al. (2024) 在 zero-shot NER 中使用 SC 获得正面结果，与本文负面结果形成有意义的对比（zero-shot vs fine-tuned、annotation selection vs output selection），遗漏可能被 reviewer 视为 related work 不完整。\n- 建议：在 Section 8 \"Consistency for structured IE\" 段中加入讨论。\n\n**W4** [warning] Escape routes 的实用价值定位模糊\n- 具体位置：Section 7.5 (Table 5)、Section 9 (Discussion, lines 569–580)\n- 问题：Entity-level construction 的最佳结果是 +1.40 pp on Few-NERD（但 cost 是 N×），cross-model ensemble 是 +0.42 pp。Discussion 中论文承认 \"the trade-off (N× cost for ≈1 pp) may only justify safety-critical use\"（lines 575–576）。对于 EMNLP 社区，≈1 pp 的改进在多数应用场景下可忽略不计，且 entity-level construction 前提（degeneracy < 22%）限制了适用范围。论文应更清楚地定位这些 escape routes 是理论洞见（证明问题可解）还是实用方案。\n- 建议：明确区分 \"proof of concept\"（degeneracy 是瓶颈，绕过它可以获得增益）和 \"practical recommendation\"。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n1. **Table 1 标注不一致**：LP 标注为 \"per-sample\" 而其他为 \"consensus-proximity\"，但 ρ 列对 EM 和 VC 标注 \"n/a†\" 而 footnote † 解释 \"One score per input\"——与 Section 2.1 的 instance-level vs per-sample 区分重复，建议统一措辞。\n\n2. **Proposition 1 的 notation**：M⁺ = ŷ_MV \\ ŷ_gr 的定义假设 MV 结果是 greedy 的超集加上额外实体，但 MV 也可能删除 greedy 中的实体（频率 < θ 的实体被移除）。Proposition 1(a) 只讨论了 degenerate instances (Δ F1 = 0) 而未讨论 MV 删除正确实体的负面场景。\n\n3. **Figure 1 信息密度过高**：三个 panel 各包含多个概念层次，caption 需要 4 行才能解释。建议拆分或简化，使 key message 更直观。\n\n4. **Appendix 体量**：附录从第 14 页延伸到第 47 页（约 34 页），远超正文。部分分析（如 Appendix g diversity analysis、Appendix e bootstrap CI）对支撑核心 claim 至关重要，建议将最关键的证据整合到正文。\n\n5. **引用格式小问题**：verify_references 工具检测到 snell2025scaling 的 bib key 用姓氏 \"snell\" 但 API 匹配到 first name \"charlie\"，这是 bib 格式常见问题，不影响正确性。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **诊断框架设计优秀**：四阶段 cascade（QE → degeneracy → agreement fidelity → LP compression）提供了系统化的诊断路径，超越了简单的 \"X doesn't work\" 负面结果。Proposition 1 的 degeneracy decomposition 给出了理论上界，实验验证了 bound 的 tightness（3–12% of actual gains, Section 6.3, Figure 9）。\n\n2. **实验设计全面且统计处理严谨**：4 个 NER benchmarks 覆盖不同难度梯度，9 种方法类别的系统比较，TOST 等价检验（Appendix g）、Holm-Bonferroni 校正（Appendix f）、bootstrap CI（Appendix e）等统计工具的使用体现了高标准的实证研究。\n\n3. **Introspection gap 是有趣的发现**：hidden-state probe（AUROC 0.63–0.88）超越 prompt-based self-judgment（0.51）但低于 multi-sample agreement（0.87, 仅在最简单的 CoNLL 上），揭示了模型内部表示包含 prompt 接口无法访问的质量信息（Section 3, lines 241–267）。\n\n4. **写作质量高**：术语一致，叙事逻辑清晰（从问题定义 → 诊断 → 失败分析 → 逃逸路径），信息密度大但不混乱。Limitations 坦诚且具体。\n\n5. **对社区的实际指导价值**：论文明确了在何种条件下 SC 可能有效（degeneracy < 22%、足够的 oracle headroom）、何种条件下注定失败（degeneracy > 70%），这对从业者选择推理策略有直接参考价值。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References（建议引用）\n\n1. **Ma, Wang, Sun & Li (2024). \"Self-Improving for Zero-Shot Named Entity Recognition with Large Language Models.\" NAACL 2024.** — 直接使用 SC 进行 NER，获得正面结果，是本文最相关的先行工作之一。\n\n2. **Madaan, Tandon, Gupta et al. (2023). \"Self-Refine: Iterative Refinement with Self-Feedback.\" NeurIPS 2023.** — 本文的 MRSC 方法与 Self-Refine 的 iterative refinement 范式直接相关。\n\n3. **Wei, Wang, Schuurmans et al. (2022). \"Chain-of-Thought Prompting Elicits Reasoning in Large Language Models.\" NeurIPS 2022.** — SC 的前置工作（CoT），虽然 Wang et al. 2023 已引用，但 CoT 作为 SC 的基础应被 acknowledge。\n\n4. **Survey: \"Test-time Scaling of LLMs: A Survey from A Subproblem Structure Perspective\" (2025, arXiv:2511.14772)** — 本文的 §6.2 讨论了 scaling behavior，该 survey 提供了更完整的 TTS 分类框架，有助于定位本文的 inverse scaling 发现。\n\n---\n\n## 总结\n\n这是一篇执行质量高的诊断性研究，提出了有用的 correlation-selection gap 概念和四阶段诊断框架。主要弱点在于：(1) 核心统计结果在严格校正下不稳健；(2) 概念新颖性相对 MBR 文献的增量定位不够清晰；(3) escape routes 的实用价值有限。论文适合 Findings 级别发表，如果能加强 N1（统计稳健性）和 N2（新颖性定位）的论证，有可能提升到 Borderline Conference。",
```

```json
    "text": "## Reviewer 2 审稿报告：新颖性 + 完整性\n\n**论文**: \"The Capability Ceiling of Self-Consistency: How Entity-Level Self-Verification Fails in Named Entity Recognition\"\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR)\n**类别**: Main Conference, Long Paper\n\n---\n\n## 总体评分\n\n**Overall**: 3.5 — Borderline Conference\n**一句话总结**: 一篇执行扎实的负面结果诊断研究，提出了有用的\"correlation-selection gap\"概念框架，但核心发现的新颖性有限（SC 对结构化预测效果差在 MBR 文献中已有先兆），且关键统计结果在严格多重比较校正下未能存活。\n\n---\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 方法论整体严谨（Holm-Bonferroni 校正、bootstrap CI、TOST 等价检验），但核心 claim 在全局校正下 p_adj=0.055 未达显著；SciERC 仅 n=529 且 seed 敏感性 σ=0.48 pp；部分 escape route 增益在噪声范围内 |\n| Excitement | 3/5 | 诊断框架（4-stage cascade）是有用的概念贡献，但\"SC 对结构化输出效果差\"对熟悉 MBR 文献的读者并不意外；escape routes 增益微弱（+1.0–1.4 pp）；负面结果价值中等 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 实验细节充分，checklist 完整，承诺代码开源；超参数、训练配置、评估协议均有记录 |\n| Overall Assessment | 3.5/5 | Borderline Conference — 执行质量高但新颖性中等，更适合 Findings |\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] 核心统计 claim 的稳健性不足\n- 具体位置：Section 7.5 (Table 5)、Appendix f (Table 13)\n- 问题：论文承认在全局 Holm-Bonferroni 校正（k=57）下，\"no core NER claim survives\"（p_adj=0.055 for Few-NERD LP selection and construction）。论文转而报告 per-family 校正（selection k=21, construction k=10），但 per-family 分割本身是事后决定，削弱了统计严谨性。Few-NERD LP selection 的 per-family p_adj=0.021 是唯一正面结果，而 SciERC construction 的 p_adj=0.20 不显著。\n- 为什么影响接收：论文 4 个 contribution 中的第 4 个（\"prescriptive escape routes\"）依赖这些统计结果。如果核心正面结果在合理的多重比较校正下不显著，那么\"escape routes\"的贡献就被大幅削弱，论文变成纯负面结果 + 诊断框架。\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 在正文中明确讨论全局 vs per-family 校正的取舍，而非仅在 Appendix f 披露；(2) 预注册 hypothesis families 或提供更强的 justification；(3) 考虑用 permutation test 或 Bayesian 方法补充频率主义检验。\n\n**N2** [critical] \"Correlation-selection gap\" 概念的新颖性定位不够精准\n- 具体位置：Section 1 (lines 94–95)、Section 4、Section 8\n- 问题：论文将 \"correlation-selection gap\" 作为核心概念贡献，但未充分区分与 MBR 文献中已知发现的关系。Eikema & Aziz (2020, 2022) 已经证明 MAP decoding 的不足，Eikema et al. (2025) 明确指出 MBR utilities 在结构化输出上表现退化。论文的 Related Work (Section 8, lines 533–548) 讨论了 MBR 但主要聚焦于 utility function 选择，未明确论证 \"correlation-selection gap\" 相比 \"MBR utility degrade on structured outputs\" 的增量贡献。Instance-level signal 的设计性局限（4/5 信号是 instance-level 因此无法区分 per-sample 候选，Section 2.1 lines 162–173）更像是方法设计问题而非深层发现。\n- 为什么影响接收：新颖性是论文能否从 Findings 升级到 Main Conference 的关键判据。如果核心概念在先行工作中已有类似表述，贡献定位就需要更精确。\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 在 Related Work 中增加一段 explicit comparison，说明 MBR utility degradation (Eikema et al., 2025) 与 correlation-selection gap 的区别和联系；(2) 强调诊断 cascade 的增量价值——MBR 文献指出问题存在，本文定位问题在哪个环节发生（degeneracy vs. LP compression），这是真正的新贡献。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] 模型覆盖范围有限，泛化性声明过度\n- 具体位置：Section 7.1 (lines 425–431)、Limitations (lines 600–618)\n- 问题：完整实验仅覆盖 Qwen3-8B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B（均为 LoRA 微调）。72B zero-shot 扩展仅在 Few-NERD 上进行，GPT-5.5 仅 200 实例。论文在 Limitations 中承认 frontier API models（Claude Sonnet 4, DeepSeek Chat）表现出不同的 degeneracy profiles（4% vs 5.5%），\"findings from fine-tuned open-weight models may not fully generalize to proprietary systems\"（lines 619–624）。但 Introduction 和 Conclusion 的措辞（如 \"persistent gap\"、\"capability ceiling\"）暗示普遍性结论。\n- 建议：弱化泛化性措辞，明确区分 \"fine-tuned open-weight models under constrained decoding\" 和一般性结论；或增加至少一个 proprietary model 的完整实验。\n\n**W2** [warning] Cross-task validation 较薄弱\n- 具体位置：Section 1 (lines 125–126)、Appendix o\n- 问题：RE 验证仅在 SciERC 一个数据集上进行，且 RE F1 被 entity span errors 严重干扰（55.5% 的 zero-F1 RE instances 来自 span 错误，footnote 1）。SQL parsing 仅提到 87.2% degeneracy 但缺乏系统分析。论文声称 NER 是 \"broader set-valued generation problem\" 的代表（line 68），但跨任务证据不够支撑这一定位。\n- 建议：(1) 如果 RE 数据受 span 错误污染严重，应讨论这对 cross-task validation 的影响；(2) SQL parsing 需更详细的分析或降低 cross-task generalization 的 claim。\n\n**W3** [warning] 遗漏直接相关的参考文献\n- 具体位置：Section 8 (Related Work)\n- 问题：以下工作与本文高度相关但未被引用：\n  1. **Ma et al. (2024) \"Self-Improving for Zero-Shot Named Entity Recognition with Large Language Models\" (NAACL 2024)** — 直接使用 self-consistency 进行 NER annotation selection，是对 SC 在 NER 上应用的直接先行工作，论文应讨论其发现与本文的关系\n  2. **Madaan et al. (2023) \"Self-Refine: Iterative Refinement with Self-Feedback\" (NeurIPS 2023)** — 论文测试了 Multi-Round Self-Consistency (MRSC, Section 3)，但未引用最相关的 iterative self-refinement 基准工作\n- 为什么重要：Ma et al. (2024) 在 zero-shot NER 中使用 SC 获得正面结果，与本文负面结果形成有意义的对比（zero-shot vs fine-tuned、annotation selection vs output selection），遗漏可能被 reviewer 视为 related work 不完整。\n- 建议：在 Section 8 \"Consistency for structured IE\" 段中加入讨论。\n\n**W4** [warning] Escape routes 的实用价值定位模糊\n- 具体位置：Section 7.5 (Table 5)、Section 9 (Discussion, lines 569–580)\n- 问题：Entity-level construction 的最佳结果是 +1.40 pp on Few-NERD（但 cost 是 N×），cross-model ensemble 是 +0.42 pp。Discussion 中论文承认 \"the trade-off (N× cost for ≈1 pp) may only justify safety-critical use\"（lines 575–576）。对于 EMNLP 社区，≈1 pp 的改进在多数应用场景下可忽略不计，且 entity-level construction 前提（degeneracy < 22%）限制了适用范围。论文应更清楚地定位这些 escape routes 是理论洞见（证明问题可解）还是实用方案。\n- 建议：明确区分 \"proof of concept\"（degeneracy 是瓶颈，绕过它可以获得增益）和 \"practical recommendation\"。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n1. **Table 1 标注不一致**：LP 标注为 \"per-sample\" 而其他为 \"consensus-proximity\"，但 ρ 列对 EM 和 VC 标注 \"n/a†\" 而 footnote † 解释 \"One score per input\"——与 Section 2.1 的 instance-level vs per-sample 区分重复，建议统一措辞。\n\n2. **Proposition 1 的 notation**：M⁺ = ŷ_MV \\ ŷ_gr 的定义假设 MV 结果是 greedy 的超集加上额外实体，但 MV 也可能删除 greedy 中的实体（频率 < θ 的实体被移除）。Proposition 1(a) 只讨论了 degenerate instances (Δ F1 = 0) 而未讨论 MV 删除正确实体的负面场景。\n\n3. **Figure 1 信息密度过高**：三个 panel 各包含多个概念层次，caption 需要 4 行才能解释。建议拆分或简化，使 key message 更直观。\n\n4. **Appendix 体量**：附录从第 14 页延伸到第 47 页（约 34 页），远超正文。部分分析（如 Appendix g diversity analysis、Appendix e bootstrap CI）对支撑核心 claim 至关重要，建议将最关键的证据整合到正文。\n\n5. **引用格式小问题**：verify_references 工具检测到 snell2025scaling 的 bib key 用姓氏 \"snell\" 但 API 匹配到 first name \"charlie\"，这是 bib 格式常见问题，不影响正确性。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **诊断框架设计优秀**：四阶段 cascade（QE → degeneracy → agreement fidelity → LP compression）提供了系统化的诊断路径，超越了简单的 \"X doesn't work\" 负面结果。Proposition 1 的 degeneracy decomposition 给出了理论上界，实验验证了 bound 的 tightness（3–12% of actual gains, Section 6.3, Figure 9）。\n\n2. **实验设计全面且统计处理严谨**：4 个 NER benchmarks 覆盖不同难度梯度，9 种方法类别的系统比较，TOST 等价检验（Appendix g）、Holm-Bonferroni 校正（Appendix f）、bootstrap CI（Appendix e）等统计工具的使用体现了高标准的实证研究。\n\n3. **Introspection gap 是有趣的发现**：hidden-state probe（AUROC 0.63–0.88）超越 prompt-based self-judgment（0.51）但低于 multi-sample agreement（0.87, 仅在最简单的 CoNLL 上），揭示了模型内部表示包含 prompt 接口无法访问的质量信息（Section 3, lines 241–267）。\n\n4. **写作质量高**：术语一致，叙事逻辑清晰（从问题定义 → 诊断 → 失败分析 → 逃逸路径），信息密度大但不混乱。Limitations 坦诚且具体。\n\n5. **对社区的实际指导价值**：论文明确了在何种条件下 SC 可能有效（degeneracy < 22%、足够的 oracle headroom）、何种条件下注定失败（degeneracy > 70%），这对从业者选择推理策略有直接参考价值。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References（建议引用）\n\n1. **Ma, Wang, Sun & Li (2024). \"Self-Improving for Zero-Shot Named Entity Recognition with Large Language Models.\" NAACL 2024.** — 直接使用 SC 进行 NER，获得正面结果，是本文最相关的先行工作之一。\n\n2. **Madaan, Tandon, Gupta et al. (2023). \"Self-Refine: Iterative Refinement with Self-Feedback.\" NeurIPS 2023.** — 本文的 MRSC 方法与 Self-Refine 的 iterative refinement 范式直接相关。\n\n3. **Wei, Wang, Schuurmans et al. (2022). \"Chain-of-Thought Prompting Elicits Reasoning in Large Language Models.\" NeurIPS 2022.** — SC 的前置工作（CoT），虽然 Wang et al. 2023 已引用，但 CoT 作为 SC 的基础应被 acknowledge。\n\n4. **Survey: \"Test-time Scaling of LLMs: A Survey from A Subproblem Structure Perspective\" (2025, arXiv:2511.14772)** — 本文的 §6.2 讨论了 scaling behavior，该 survey 提供了更完整的 TTS 分类框架，有助于定位本文的 inverse scaling 发现。\n\n---\n\n## 总结\n\n这是一篇执行质量高的诊断性研究，提出了有用的 correlation-selection gap 概念和四阶段诊断框架。主要弱点在于：(1) 核心统计结果在严格校正下不稳健；(2) 概念新颖性相对 MBR 文献的增量定位不够清晰；(3) escape routes 的实用价值有限。论文适合 Findings 级别发表，如果能加强 N1（统计稳健性）和 N2（新颖性定位）的论证，有可能提升到 Borderline Conference。",
    "reviewer_id": "2",

  }
}
```

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见，请逐条回应。对每个 Must-Fix 和 Should-Fix 问题，标注 [AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

---

## Reviewer 1 的 Must-Fix 问题

**N1** [critical] 诊断级联的因果推断力度不足，与标题"Capability Ceiling"的强 claim 不匹配
- §3-4 (诊断框架), Limitations (p.8)
- 四阶段级联基于观察性关联建立，但标题声称"capability ceiling"。Figure 1 caption 标注"diagnostic sequence, not causal chain"，但正文叙事隐含因果方向性。Limitations 承认"not interventional"，但被埋在末尾。
- 可能存在未测试的干预（diversity-encouraging decoding、contrastive sampling）能同时降低 degeneracy 和拓宽 LP range。
- 建议：(1) 在 §1/§3 明确声明 observational 而非 causal；(2) 改标题或提供 controlled intervention。

**N2** [critical] Free-form decoding 消融实验覆盖不完整，削弱 LP compression 作为独立瓶颈的论证
- Appendix q (p.27), Table 47-48
- LP compression 被诊断为第四阶段瓶颈，但 free-form decoding 消融仅在 CoNLL 和 Few-NERD INTRA 上执行，未覆盖 SciERC。SciERC 是 construction gain 最大的数据集 (+1.40pp, Table 2)。Table 22 (p.23) 报告 SciERC LP range 仅 0.041 nats（constrained decoding 下）。
- 如果 SciERC 在 free-form 下 selection 能改善，则 LP compression 结论对该关键数据集不成立。
- 建议：补充 SciERC free-form decoding 实验。

## Reviewer 1 的 Should-Fix 问题

**W1** [warning] 理论贡献（MBR 等价、degeneracy bound）实际预测力有限
- §6.3, Corollary 1, Table 30 (p.26/41)
- Bound 仅捕获实际 gain 的 5-12%（actual/bound ratio 3-12×），signal alignment α 是瓶颈（best signal 仅恢复 6.8% of non-degenerate oracle gap）。MBR 框架提供 qualitative insight 但 quantitative bound 过松。

**W2** [warning] Hidden-state probe 跨数据集方差未充分解释
- §3 (p.3-4), Appendix h.3 (p.21)
- AUROC 从 SciERC 0.63 到 CoNLL 0.88 变化剧烈。Cross-dataset transfer drops。未分析什么让 CoNLL hidden states 更可分。CoNLL 上 AUROC=0.88 但似乎仍不能改善 selection——需解释为什么高 AUROC 不转化为 selection gain。

**W3** [warning] Escape routes 效果量可能缺乏实际意义
- §5, Table 2, Table 61 (p.46)
- +1.02-1.40pp 需 N× 推理成本，SciERC greedy F1 ~0.529 提升到 ~0.543。未量化 compute-accuracy Pareto 曲线。

**W4** [warning] N-scaling 范围对负面结论支撑力有限
- §6.1 (p.6), Appendix s (p.28), Figure 10
- N 止步于 32。引用 Brown et al. (2024) 但未讨论 N=100+ 时 degeneracy 是否显著下降。应基于 scaling law 外推讨论。

**W5** [warning] Cross-model ensemble 仅测试 2 个模型族
- §5.1, Table 2
- Qwen3-8B + LLaMA-3.1-8B 共享 LoRA 训练流程，可能限制 diversity 上限。未讨论架构差异更大的组合。

## Reviewer 1 的小问题
- M1: Table 1 caption "One score per input" 与 LP footnote 矛盾
- M2: §2.2 定义4种信号但 LP 在 §2.3 才引入，应统一
- M3: Appendix f 全局校正导致 claims 不显著但正文 §7.5 用 per-family 校正，差异未充分说明
- M4: 引用验证问题（lightman2024verify, aggarwal2023adaptive 等）
- M5: Figure 1 右面板 "+1.4pp (Few-NERD)" 可能与 Table 2 不一致（+1.40pp 是 SciERC）
- M6: 47 页附录，核心 findings 依赖附录表格

## Reviewer 1 的主要优势评价
1. 实验覆盖深度和广度是该领域最全面的
2. 统计方法论远高于 NLP 论文平均水平
3. MBR 理论联系具有原创性
4. 诊断框架结构性洞察有持久价值
5. Cross-task 验证增强普适性
6. Failure mode gallery 提供定性理解

```json
{
  "text": "## Reviewer 2 对 Reviewer 1 审稿意见的逐条回应\n\n---\n\n### Must-Fix 问题\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N1: 诊断级联的因果推断力度不足，与标题\"Capability Ceiling\"的强 claim 不匹配**\n\n同意这是一个需要处理的问题。论文在 Figure 1 caption 明确标注 \"diagnostic sequence, not causal chain\"，Limitations（lines 590–598）也承认 \"Our evidence is correlational and theoretical (MBR framework, conditional decomposition), not interventional: we have not performed controlled interventions (e.g., artificially varying degeneracy while holding other factors constant), and the cascade stages interact in ways that resist decomposition into independent causal effects (R²<0; §4)\"。\n\n但正如 R1 所指出，这些 caveat 与标题 \"Capability Ceiling\" 的强断言之间存在张力。\"Ceiling\" 暗示一个硬性的、被证明的上界，而论文实际提供的是 observational diagnostics + loose theoretical bound（actual/bound ratio 仅 3–12%，Table 30）。建议作者在 Section 1 的 contribution 列表中明确标注诊断框架的 observational 性质，而非仅在 Limitations 中披露。\n\n不过，我认为 R1 建议的 \"provide controlled intervention\" 对本文来说可能过于苛刻——人为控制 degeneracy 同时保持其他因素不变在 LLM 实验中极难实现。论文的 LoRA-rank ablation（§7.4，将 degeneracy 从 17.8% 降至 7.2%）和 temperature ablation（Appendix u，Table 43：degeneracy 从 41.4% 到 14.9%）已经提供了有限的 quasi-interventional 证据。标题修改或措辞弱化是更可行的修改方向。\n\n**严重程度判断**：同意为 Must-Fix，但建议修改路径以标题/措辞调整为主，不需要新实验。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT] R1-N2: Free-form decoding 消融实验覆盖不完整，未覆盖 SciERC**\n\nR1 的事实判断有误。Appendix q（p.27）的 Table 32 明确标题为 \"Comparison of constrained vs. free-form decoding **on SciERC** (Qwen3-8B, N=8, T=1.0, seed=42; RE selection gap replicates across 4 seeds: +1.84±0.12 pp)\"。该表包含四个 Panel：\n\n- **Panel A**（LP 分布）：constrained tied fraction 59.7% vs free-form 57.7%，Δ = −2.0 pp\n- **Panel B**（Selection F1）：NER LP-selected 0.6241 (constrained) vs 0.6222 (free-form)，selection gap 分别为 −0.0059 和 −0.0041——两种解码方式下 NER selection 均无法改善\n- **Panel C**（QE ρ）：所有信号在 free-form 下均有微弱提升（SJ +0.018, FK +0.053），但 LP 提升仅 +0.008\n- **Panel D**（Parse rate）：free-form 解析率 99.95%\n\n论文正文 Appendix q 进一步在 lines 2184–2200 补充了 CoNLL 的 free-form 结果作为扩展验证。因此，SciERC 是 free-form ablation 的**首要**数据集，而非被遗漏。\n\n此外，lines 2056–2073 讨论了 SciERC free-form 下的 non-tied subset reshuffling：87 instances 新获 LP discrimination 但 \"aggregate NER selection gains remain negligible\"（line 2066），明确回应了 \"如果 SciERC 在 free-form 下 selection 能改善\" 的假设——答案是不能。\n\n**结论**：R1-N2 基于对论文内容的误读，不构成有效的 Must-Fix 问题。论文在 SciERC 上的 free-form 消融是完整的，且结果支持 LP compression 作为独立瓶颈的论证（tied fraction 仅减少 2 pp，selection 依然无效）。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix 问题\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W1: 理论贡献（MBR 等价、degeneracy bound）实际预测力有限**\n\n同意。Table 30 显示 actual/bound ratio 仅 3–12%，α（signal alignment）是真正的瓶颈（best signal 恢复非退化 oracle gap 的 6.8%），而非 degeneracy D。论文自身在 lines 389–392 承认 \"α—not D—is the bottleneck...the bound cannot be tightened by signal combination (§5.2) or verifier-based construction (§5.2)\"。\n\n然而，我认为理论贡献的价值不完全在于 bound 的 tightness。Corollary 1 的 θ_crit = 0.70 预测了 degeneracy 超过何种水平时增益消失（§6.3），Table 30 验证了所有正增益配置的 D < θ_crit。这提供了**定性的预测力**——告诉从业者在什么条件下不值得尝试 SC，即使 bound 本身很松。\n\n**补充发现**：我在 Round 1 审稿中也指出了类似问题——bound 松弛意味着 escape routes 的理论基础薄弱。建议作者在正文中明确将 degeneracy bound 定位为 **necessary condition**（D < θ_crit 是必要条件）而非 sufficient condition，避免读者误解为紧界。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W2: Hidden-state probe 跨数据集方差未充分解释**\n\n同意这是一个值得补充解释的问题。AUROC 从 SciERC 0.630 到 CoNLL 0.882 的变化确实需要更多分析。论文在 lines 248–267 讨论了 cross-dataset transfer 下降（LODO AUROC 0.56–0.67），并指出 \"even direct hidden-state access would not yield a universal quality signal\"（lines 265–266），但未解释**为什么** CoNLL 的 hidden states 更可分。\n\n可能的解释方向（论文未提供）：CoNLL 任务更简单（greedy F1 ≈ 91%），错误实体在 hidden space 中可能表现为更明显的 outlier；SciERC 有 6 种实体类型且 F1 仅 ≈ 65%，错误模式更多样化，增加了 hidden-state 分类难度。这与论文在 Appendix d 中发现的 \"structural signals scale with structural complexity\"（SciERC 6+ entities: ρ=0.547 vs 1–2 entities: ρ=0.415）一致。\n\n关于 \"为什么高 AUROC 不转化为 selection gain\"：这在 Section 2.1（lines 162–173）中有结构性解释——hidden-state probe 是 instance-level signal（对同一 input 的所有 N 个 candidate 产生相同 score），因此无法 discriminate per-sample candidates。但 R1 的关切更深层：即使在 CoNLL 上 AUROC=0.88 使 quality estimation 非常准确，instance-level 的设计限制仍然阻止了 selection 改善，这恰恰印证了 correlation-selection gap 的核心论点。建议作者在 §3 补充这一点作为 hidden-state probe 结果的 explicit takeaway。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W3: Escape routes 效果量可能缺乏实际意义**\n\n同意，且这与我在 Round 1 的 W4 一致。Table 61（p.46）提供了成本-效益汇总：SC + construction（N×成本）仅获 +1.02–1.40 pp，cross-model SC（2N×成本）获 +0.42 pp。论文在 Discussion（lines 575–576）承认 \"the trade-off (N× cost for ≈1 pp) may only justify safety-critical use\"。\n\n缺少的是 Pareto 曲线：在什么 N 值下达到边际收益拐点？Table 37（p.28）的 N-scaling 数据（N=2 到 32）可以绘制 cost-accuracy trade-off，但论文未这样呈现。建议作者补充这一视觉化。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W4: N-scaling 范围对负面结论支撑力有限**\n\n部分同意。Table 37 显示在 N=32 时 degeneracy 降至 4%、oracle headroom 达 +20.0 pp，但 best selection Δ 仅 +0.95 pp（LP, p=0.091）。这已经是一个很强的论据：**即使 degeneracy 几乎被消除，selection 依然失败**。\n\n然而，R1 关于 N=100+ 的 concern 有一定道理。论文引用了 Brown et al. (2024) 的 scaling law F₁(N) = a − b·N^{−c}，Figure 10 拟合了 R² > 0.999 的曲线。SciERC c ≈ 0.35 意味着 oracle headroom 的增长是 sublinear 的（从 N=32 到 N=128 oracle headroom 可能从 +20 pp 增至约 +25 pp），但论文未讨论 selection gap 是否会在更大 N 下因为候选池质量提升而缩小。\n\n**补充证据**：Table 38（N=32 bootstrap significance test）显示 LP p_greedy = 0.091，CI [−0.41, +2.34]——95% CI 跨零，即使在 N=32 时也无法排除零效应。如果 selection gap 在 N=32 时都无法达到显著，继续增大 N 改善 selection 的概率很低（因为瓶颈是 signal alignment α 而非 candidate pool size）。建议论文在 Appendix s 增加基于 scaling law 的 extrapolation 讨论。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W5: Cross-model ensemble 仅测试 2 个模型族**\n\n同意，且与我的 W1 一致。Qwen3-8B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B 共享相同的 LoRA 训练流程（LLaMA Factory），可能限制了 diversity 上限。论文的 frontier model 实验（Table 41）显示不同架构间 degeneracy 差异巨大（Claude Sonnet 4: 74%, DeepSeek: 5.5%），暗示更多样化的 ensemble 可能产生更大的增益，但这未被验证。\n\n---\n\n### 小问题回应\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M1**: Table 1 caption 的 \"†One score per input\" 确实容易与 per-sample/instance-level 区分混淆。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M2**: §2.2 先定义 4 种信号再在 §2.3 引入 LP，结构上可以改善。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M3**: Appendix f 全局校正 vs §7.5 per-family 校正的差异——这也是我 Round 1 审稿的 N1。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-M4**: 引用验证工具检测到 lightman2024verify、wang2023selfconsistency、aggarwal2023adaptive 为 \"not_found\"，但这是 OpenAlex API 的覆盖局限（这三篇都是知名且真实的论文），不构成引用问题。snell2025scaling 和 dembczynski2011exact 的 \"mismatch\" 是 LaTeX 编码导致的姓氏格式差异（Müller vs M\\\"uller, Dembczyński vs Dembczy\\'nski），属于 bib 文件的正常现象。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-M5**: R1 称 \"Figure 1 右面板 '+1.4pp (Few-NERD)' 可能与 Table 2 不一致（+1.40pp 是 SciERC）\"。实际上 Table 5 明确显示 **Few-NERD** construction Δ = +1.40±0.51 pp，**SciERC** 为 +1.02±0.49 pp。Figure 1 的 \"+1.4 pp (Few-NERD; §7.5)\" 与 Table 5 完全一致。R1 混淆了两个数据集的数字。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M6**: 47 页附录体量过大——与我 Round 1 小问题第 4 条一致。\n\n---\n\n### 综合评估\n\nR1 的审稿整体质量高，对诊断框架的因果推断局限性（N1）和理论 bound 的松弛性（W1）的指出均有价值。但 **N2（free-form decoding 缺失 SciERC）是一个事实性错误**——Table 32 明确在 SciERC 上执行了 free-form ablation，结果支持论文的结论。M5 同样是事实性误读。\n\n去除 N2 后，R1 的 Must-Fix 仅剩 N1（因果性/标题措辞），这可以通过修改标题和增加 Section 1 的 caveat 来解决，不需要新实验。R1 的 Should-Fix 问题（W1–W5）与我 Round 1 的发现高度一致，支持论文处于 **3.5 Borderline Conference** 的评分区间。",
  "reviewer_id": "2",
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie"
}
```

use\"。\n\n缺少的是 Pareto 曲线：在什么 N 值下达到边际收益拐点？Table 37（p.28）的 N-scaling 数据（N=2 到 32）可以绘制 cost-accuracy trade-off，但论文未这样呈现。建议作者补充这一视觉化。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W4: N-scaling 范围对负面结论支撑力有限**\n\n部分同意。Table 37 显示在 N=32 时 degeneracy 降至 4%、oracle headroom 达 +20.0 pp，但 best selection Δ 仅 +0.95 pp（LP, p=0.091）。这已经是一个很强的论据：**即使 degeneracy 几乎被消除，selection 依然失败**。\n\n然而，R1 关于 N=100+ 的 concern 有一定道理。论文引用了 Brown et al. (2024) 的 scaling law F₁(N) = a − b·N^{−c}，Figure 10 拟合了 R² > 0.999 的曲线。SciERC c ≈ 0.35 意味着 oracle headroom 的增长是 sublinear 的（从 N=32 到 N=128 oracle headroom 可能从 +20 pp 增至约 +25 pp），但论文未讨论 selection gap 是否会在更大 N 下因为候选池质量提升而缩小。\n\n**补充证据**：Table 38（N=32 bootstrap significance test）显示 LP p_greedy = 0.091，CI [−0.41, +2.34]——95% CI 跨零，即使在 N=32 时也无法排除零效应。如果 selection gap 在 N=32 时都无法达到显著，继续增大 N 改善 selection 的概率很低（因为瓶颈是 signal alignment α 而非 candidate pool size）。建议论文在 Appendix s 增加基于 scaling law 的 extrapolation 讨论。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W5: Cross-model ensemble 仅测试 2 个模型族**\n\n同意，且与我的 W1 一致。Qwen3-8B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B 共享相同的 LoRA 训练流程（LLaMA Factory），可能限制了 diversity 上限。论文的 frontier model 实验（Table 41）显示不同架构间 degeneracy 差异巨大（Claude Sonnet 4: 74%, DeepSeek: 5.5%），暗示更多样化的 ensemble 可能产生更大的增益，但这未被验证。\n\n---\n\n### 小问题回应\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M1**: Table 1 caption 的 \"†One score per input\" 确实容易与 per-sample/instance-level 区分混淆。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M2**: §2.2 先定义 4 种信号再在 §2.3 引入 LP，结构上可以改善。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M3**: Appendix f 全局校正 vs §7.5 per-family 校正的差异——这也是我 Round 1 审稿的 N1。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-M4**: 引用验证工具检测到 lightman2024verify、wang2023selfconsistency、aggarwal2023adaptive 为 \"not_found\"，但这是 OpenAlex API 的覆盖局限（这三篇都是知名且真实的论文），不构成引用问题。snell2025scaling 和 dembczynski2011exact 的 \"mismatch\" 是 LaTeX 编码导致的姓氏格式差异（Müller vs M\\\"uller, Dembczyński vs Dembczy\\'nski），属于 bib 文件的正常现象。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-M5**: R1 称 \"Figure 1 右面板 '+1.4pp (Few-NERD)' 可能与 Table 2 不一致（+1.40pp 是 SciERC）\"。实际上 Table 5 明确显示 **Few-NERD** construction Δ = +1.40±0.51 pp，**SciERC** 为 +1.02±0.49 pp。Figure 1 的 \"+1.4 pp (Few-NERD; §7.5)\" 与 Table 5 完全一致。R1 混淆了两个数据集的数字。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M6**: 47 页附录体量过大——与我 Round 1 小问题第 4 条一致。\n\n---\n\n### 综合评估\n\nR1 的审稿整体质量高，对诊断框架的因果推断局限性（N1）和理论 bound 的松弛性（W1）的指出均有价值。但 **N2（free-form decoding 缺失 SciERC）是一个事实性错误**——Table 32 明确在 SciERC 上执行了 free-form ablation，结果支持论文的结论。M5 同样是事实性误读。\n\n去除 N2 后，R1 的 Must-Fix 仅剩 N1（因果性/标题措辞），这可以通过修改标题和增加 Section 1 的 caveat 来解决，不需要新实验。R1 的 Should-Fix 问题（W1–W5）与我 Round 1 的发现高度一致，支持论文处于 **3.5 Borderline Conference** 的评分区间。",
```json
    "reviewer_id": "2",
    "project": "struct_self_consist_ie"
  }
}
```

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责**新颖性、framing、完整性和 Related Work**。

## Venue 信息
- **目标**: EMNLP 2026 (Long Paper, ARR)
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25
- **正文页数**: ≤8 页（ACL LaTeX 模板，`\usepackage[review]{acl}`）
- **参考文献**: 不限页数
- **Limitations/Ethics**: 不计入页数
- **匿名规则**: Double-blind
- **评分标准**: ARR Overall Assessment（5=Award, 4=Conference, 3=Findings, 2=Resubmit, 1=Do not resubmit）

## 论文路径
- LaTeX: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.tex`
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.pdf`

## 论文概要
标题: "Characterizing the Capability Ceiling of Self-Consistency: How Entity-Level Verification Fails in NER"
核心论点: NER 中 entity-level agreement signals 能做到 quality estimation（ρ up to 0.47, AUROC up to 0.82）但无法做到 sample selection（所有 signal 都不优于 greedy decoding），称之为 "correlation-selection gap"。论文提出四阶段诊断 cascade 定位失败原因，并给出三条绕过路径（LP selection, entity construction, cross-model ensemble）。

## 你的审查侧重

### 必须覆盖
1. **新颖性评估**:
   - "correlation-selection gap" 这一概念是否真正新颖，还是 MBR 文献（Eikema & Aziz 2020/2022, Eikema et al. 2025）已知发现的重新包装
   - 四阶段诊断 cascade 的增量贡献 vs 已有 SC failure mode 文献（Chen et al. 2023, Huang et al. 2024）
   - Entity construction 作为 contribution 是否足够新颖（vs 标准 MBR / ensemble 方法）
   - Hidden-state probe 结果（AUROC 0.63-0.88）的新颖性 vs 已知 probe 文献
   - 整体贡献量：论文同时做诊断框架 + 9 methods 评估 + 理论分析 + prescriptive，每项深度是否足够

2. **Framing 审查**:
   - 标题 "Capability Ceiling" 是否与 observational 证据匹配（论文承认 "not interventional"）
   - 因果语言 vs 相关性证据的一致性（"governs", "explaining where and why" 等措辞）
   - Abstract 信息密度是否过高
   - Contributions 的定位（diagnostic framework vs prescriptive escape routes 的主次关系）
   - NER-centric 证据 vs broader structured prediction claims 的 scope 匹配

3. **Related Work 完整性**:
   - 与 MBR 文献的差异化是否充分
   - 与 Yao et al. (2026), Yun et al. (2025) 的区分是否清晰
   - 是否遗漏重要先行工作（特别是 SC-for-NER: Ma et al. 2024 NAACL; self-refine: Madaan et al. 2023 NeurIPS）
   - 引用质量和完整性（orphan bib entries, 元数据）
   - Consensus decoding 文献（DeNero et al. 2009）是否引用

4. **论证完整性**:
   - RE extension（55.5% zero-F1, span error confound）是否应作为 cross-task validation 呈现
   - SQL 分析（仅 n=196, 87.2% degeneracy, 无系统分析）是否足够
   - Cross-model results 仅 1 数据集 1 模型对——是否足以支撑 contribution
   - Limitations section 是否充分覆盖核心局限

5. **写作质量**:
   - 术语定义一致性
   - Abstract 中缩写处理
   - Figure/Table caption 的自含性

### 输出格式
请按以下结构输出审稿意见:
1. **Summary** (2-3句)
2. **Strengths** (逐条, S1/S2/...)
3. **Weaknesses** (逐条, W1/W2/..., 标注 severity: critical/warning/info)
4. **Questions** (Q1/Q2/...)
5. **Missing References** (如有)
6. **Minor Issues** (M1/M2/...)
7. **Overall Assessment** (评分 + 置信度 + 1句总评)

严格客观，不留情面。

_source line 2951 · 2026-05-21T16:56:22.712349+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Scoring Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence**
Scale: 1-5
- 5: Positive evaluation is correct; careful reading and familiarity with related work
- 4: Quite sure; checked important points carefully
- 3: Pretty sure; good general knowledge but didn't verify all details
- 2: Can defend evaluation but likely missed some details or novelty assessment
- 1: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand"

## 2. **Soundness**
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- 5: "one of the most thorough" studies observed
- 4: "sufficient support for all claims"; extra experiments optional
- 3: "sufficient support for main claims"; minor details need work
- 2: Main claims lack support; major technical/methodological problems
- 1: "not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

## 3. **Excitement**
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
Measures subjective appeal regarding innovation, impact, and relevance
- 5: Would recommend to others; would attend presentation
- 4: Would mention to others; effort to attend
- 3: Might mention points; attend if time permits
- 2: "does not resonate with me, but might with others"
- 1: No resonance; unlikely appeal to broader community

## 4. **Overall Assessment**
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- 5: Consider for Award (top 2.5% papers)
- 4: Conference acceptance
- 3: Accept to Findings track
- 2: Resubmit next cycle
- 1: Do not resubmit

## 5. **Additional Dimensions**
- Reproducibility (1-5 scale)
- Dataset value (1-5 scale)
- Software value (1-5 scale)
- Author identity knowledge (1-5 scale)

Based on the search results, I found relevant information about self-consistency in NER and related structured prediction challenges:

## Self-Consistency in NER

Self-consistency methods discard prompts with inconsistent predictions in the context of NER tasks. Self-consistency (SC) is applied to obtain a SC score for each prediction, which can be used for reliable annotation selection in zero-shot NER scenarios with large language models.

## Entity-Level Consistency Challenges

The search results reveal significant challenges with entity-level consistency in NER:

The use of adjectives and prepositions within entities may be contributing to low label consistency. Inconsistent predictions can lead to significant errors in information extraction and knowledge discovery in biomedical applications.

```json
{
```
```json
  "text": "## Reviewer 2 审稿意见（Round 1）\n\n**论文**: \"Characterizing the Capability Ceiling of Self-Consistency: How Entity-Level Verification Fails in NER\"\n**审查侧重**: 新颖性、framing、完整性、Related Work\n\n---\n\n### 1. Summary\n\n本文研究 NER 中 entity-level 自一致性信号的能力边界。核心发现：agreement-based 信号能估计输出质量（ρ up to 0.47, AUROC up to 0.82）但无法用于 sample selection（所有信号均不优于 greedy），称为 \"correlation-selection gap\"。提出四阶段诊断 cascade + 三条绕过路径（LP selection, entity construction, cross-model ensemble）。4 个 NER 数据集、2 个 model family、9 种方法，附 hidden-state probe 和 MBR 理论。\n\n### 2. Strengths\n\n**S1.** 系统性负面结果：4 datasets × 2 model families × 9 methods 一致证明 entity-level SC selection 失败（Table 1, 2），统计检验充分（Holm-Bonferroni, bootstrap CI, permutation tests）。\n\n**S2.** 诊断 cascade 结构化分解失败原因（degeneracy → agreement fidelity → LP compression），比单纯报告\"SC 不 work\"有更多 insight。\n\n**S3.** MBR 理论连接严谨（Appendix p.1-p.2, Proposition 1-2），与 consensus decoding 文献清晰关联。\n\n**S4.** 实验设计完整：温度敏感性、N-scaling、free-form decoding ablation、robustness 多维验证。\n\n**S5.** Hidden-state probe（AUROC 0.63-0.88）提供独立 introspection boundary 证据。\n\n### 3. Weaknesses\n\n**W1 [critical] \"Capability Ceiling\" 标题与 observational 证据不匹配。** 论文自承 \"correlational and theoretical, not interventional\"（Limitations），但标题暗示 impossibility result。主文多处因果措辞（\"governs\"、\"determines\"、\"explaining where and why\"）与 observational design 矛盾。理论结果（Proposition 1）依赖 entity-wise independence 假设，成立程度未严格量化。建议修改标题和系统替换因果措辞。\n\n**W2 [critical] Cross-task validation 薄弱，但 framing 暗示 broader structured prediction 适用性。** RE 仅 1 数据集（SciERC, n=529 non-zero）、55.5% zero-F1 + span error confound、信号排序与 NER 反转；SQL 仅 n=196, 87.2% degeneracy, 无系统分析；cross-model 仅 1 dataset × 1 model pair (+0.42 pp)。无法支撑 \"structured prediction\" 层面的 claim。建议将 RE/SQL 定位为 \"preliminary exploration\"，scope 限定为 NER。\n\n**W3 [warning] Correlation-selection gap 与 MBR 文献已有发现的区分不够清晰。** Eikema & Aziz (2022) 已发现需要 decouple candidate set 和 reference set；Müller & Sennrich (2021) 证明 MBR 某些条件下不优于 beam search。主文未充分讨论这些已有发现如何 inform 或 anticipate 本文的 gap。读者可能认为这是 MBR 已知现象的 re-instantiation。\n\n**W4 [warning] Degeneracy 作为核心解释存在 tautological element。** High degeneracy = 模型高度确信 → 本就不需要 selection，这是 trivial。关键问题是 SC 在 non-degenerate instances 上也失败（conditional ρ 接近 0），而这个更简洁的 narrative 不需要四阶段 cascade。\n\n**W5 [warning] Entity construction 新颖性有限。** 实质是 entity-level majority vote with threshold（θ=2/N），论文自己将其形式化为 MBR 特殊情况。+1.4 pp gain 在绝对值上不大。\n\n**W6 [warning] Abstract 信息密度过高。** ~180 词含 12+ 数字、多个首次出现的缩写、4 项 contributions，影响可读性。\n\n**W7 [warning] Limitations 未覆盖几个重要局限：** cross-task 证据弱点、cross-model 极度有限、entity construction gain 幅度、probe overfit 可能性。\n\n### 4. Questions\n\n**Q1.** LP 在 Few-NERD 上 anomalous positive gain（+0.22）的原因？与 degeneracy rate (ρ=0.20) 有关？\n**Q2.** Hidden-state probe 是否做过 leave-one-dataset-out CV 评估 transferability？\n**Q3.** Entity construction \"bypasses entity-level self-verification\" 与其本身依赖 entity-level agreement 的逻辑矛盾如何解释？\n**Q4.** Cross-model 为何未在 Few-NERD（degeneracy 最低）上测试？\n**Q5.** Proposition 1 的 entity-wise independence 假设 violation 程度如何量化？\n\n### 5. Missing References\n\n- 引用验证：57 bib entries, 0 orphan, 0 missing, 2 not found (OpenAlex coverage gaps), 4 encoding mismatches\n- **建议补充**：selective prediction NLP 近期工作（Varshney et al. 2022 ACL Findings; Xin et al. 2021）；structured uncertainty（Malinin & Gales 2021 ICLR）\n- **需核实**：ma2024selfimproving 第一作者（bib 为 \"Ma\"，OpenAlex 记录为 \"Xie\"）\n\n### 6. Minor Issues\n\nM1. Abstract 中 ρ/AUROC 来自不同信号但写法可能误导\nM2. Figure 1 caption disclaimer 与主文因果措辞矛盾\nM3. Eq. 1 引用 Hungarian matching 但未给出引用\nM4. \"GPT-5.5\" 首次出现未解释\nM5. Footnote 1 位置不当（NER context 中提 RE）\nM6. 47 页论文（10+37 appendix），大量关键证据仅在附录中\n\n### 7. Overall Assessment\n\n| 维度 | 分数 |\n|------|------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 |\n| Excitement | 3/5 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 |\n| **Overall** | **3.0 — Findings** |\n| Confidence | 4/5 |\n\n**一句话总评**：NER 范围内的系统性诊断工作有价值，但 \"capability ceiling\" framing 超出 observational 证据支撑，跨任务/跨模型证据严重不足，与 MBR 文献区分需加强。适合 Findings。",
  "reviewer_id": "2",
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie"
}
```

# Reviewer 1 审稿意见：方法严谨性、实验设计与统计分析

**论文**: "Characterizing the Capability Ceiling of Self-Consistency: How Entity-Level Verification Fails in NER"
**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (Long Paper, ARR)

## 1. Summary
本文系统研究了 NER 中 entity-level agreement signals 的能力边界，发现这些 signals 能做 quality estimation（ρ up to 0.47, AUROC up to 0.82）但无法做 sample selection（无 signal 显著优于 greedy decoding），将此称为 "correlation-selection gap"。论文提出四阶段诊断 cascade（QE → degeneracy → agreement fidelity → LP compression）定位失败原因，建立了与 MBR decoding 的理论联系（Proposition 1），并给出三条绕过路径（entity-level construction, cross-model ensemble, trained verifier）。实验覆盖 4 个 NER 数据集 + RE + SQL，6 个模型（4B-72B + GPT-5.5），9 类方法，附带 57 项统计检验的 Holm-Bonferroni 校正。

## 2. Strengths
**S1: 实验覆盖度和系统性极为出色。** 4 个 NER benchmark（SciERC/Few-NERD/CoNLL/WNUT-17）跨越 domain、entity 类型数（4-66）和难度梯度；模型从 4B 到 72B 再到 GPT-5.5 覆盖开源微调和闭源 API 两种范式；9 类方法（Table 2）涵盖 instance-level selection、entity-level construction、cross-model ensemble 等。60+ 张表格和 9 张图的 33 页 appendix 提供了罕见级别的实验细节。

**S2: 诊断框架设计精巧且可复用。** 四阶段 cascade（§3-4）逐步排除失败原因：先用三个独立 probe（ESJ §3, MRSC §3, LSC §3）确认自验证失败的普遍性，再通过 degeneracy decomposition（§6.3）分离 "所有样本相同" 的 trivial failure mode，最后定位 LP compression 为残余瓶颈。每个阶段有明确的 pass/fail 判据。这个框架对未来 structured generation 的 test-time compute 研究有方法论贡献。

**S3: 统计方法论严谨。** 全局 Holm-Bonferroni 校正覆盖 k=57 项检验（Table 13, p38），bootstrap 95% CI 用于效应量估计（Table 12），TOST 等价性检验用于支撑负面结论（Appendix p20），instance-level permutation test 验证 FK 的非 trivial 来源（Table 8, p15）。对于一篇以负面结果为核心论断的论文，这种统计审慎度是必要的，作者做到了。

**S4: 负面结果的建设性处理。** 论文不止于报告 "self-consistency 在 NER 上失败"，而是（a）诊断 WHY（degeneracy + LP compression + verification failure），（b）建立理论联系（MBR equivalence, Entity Selection Bound），（c）提出三条可操作的绕过路径（entity construction +1.02-1.40pp, cross-model +0.42pp, verifier +0.30pp; Table 61, p46）。这使论文从 "negative finding" 升级为 "diagnostic + prescriptive" 贡献。

**S5: MBR 理论联系有启发性。** 将 entity-level construction 形式化为 structured MBR decoding（Appendix p.2, §25）是一个有洞察力的连接，将 NER self-consistency 嵌入更广泛的 decoding 理论中。虽然 bound 较松（见 W2），但这个联系本身为后续工作指明了方向。

## 3. Weaknesses
**W1 [critical] SciERC 单 seed 显著削弱 25% 的 NER 证据基础**
- SciERC 仅用 1 个 random seed（seed=42），而 Few-NERD 和 CoNLL 用 3 seeds。论文核心负面论断建立在 4 个 NER 数据集上，SciERC 占 25% 权重。单 seed 意味着 selection Δ 估计无方差信息，bootstrap CI 仅反映 instance-level 变异。SciERC 是 construction gain 最大的数据集（+1.53pp ZS），单 seed 结果可能是 seed-specific artifact。
- 建议补充 SciERC 3-seed 或在正文中更显著标注 single-seed caveat。

**W2 [critical] Proposition 1 (Entity Selection Bound) 过松，理论贡献受限**
- Bound (1−D)·G_nd·α 仅捕获实际 gain 的 3-12%（Table 30）。三个因子各有意义但相乘后过于保守。论文自述 "the bound is loose"（p27），但仍列为核心贡献。
- 建议将 Proposition 1 定位为 "diagnostic decomposition" 而非 "performance bound"。

**W3 [warning] RE 和 SQL 扩展过于单薄，不足以支撑 "beyond NER" 的泛化声明**
- RE 仅 SciERC 单数据集单模型，SQL 仅 GPT-5.5 在 Spider 上简短提及（87.2% degeneracy = ceiling effect）。建议降低宣传力度或补充数据集。

**W4 [warning] 训练配方差异构成跨数据集比较的潜在混淆因素**
- Few-NERD 3 epochs vs 其他 5 epochs。SciERC 消融显示 degeneracy 随 epoch 单调递增（43.5%→60.3%），但 Few-NERD 未做 5-epoch 对照。

**W5 [warning] 负面结论的统计效力存疑**
- Cross-dataset correlation（n=23）的 post-hoc power 在 ρ=0.3 时仅约 0.32。主文措辞比证据允许的更断定性。

**W6 [warning] Degeneracy 可能是 correlation-selection gap 的主要甚至唯一解释**
- High degeneracy = trivially tied scores。Non-degenerate 分析显示 α 极低（0.000-0.161）才是真正瓶颈，但这一发现被 "correlation-selection gap" framing 所 overshadow。更精确的 framing 可能是 "near-zero selection alignment despite moderate quality correlation"。

## 4. Questions
**Q1**: SciERC 为何仅用 1 个 seed？是资源限制还是方法论考虑？
**Q2**: LP compression threshold ε=0.05 的选择依据？不同 ε 是否改变 LP ranking？
**Q3**: TOST 等价性检验的 equivalence margin δ 是如何选择的？是否 data-driven 导致循环推理？
**Q4**: Zero-shot instruction-tuned 模型下 gap 幅度是否有实质变化？
**Q5**: Cross-model ensemble 用同架构不同 checkpoint 是否也有类似增益？

## 5. Missing References
未发现重要缺失。可选补充 Malinin & Gales (2021)。

## 6. Minor Issues
M1: Table 13 caption k=52 vs k=57 需确认
M2: Figure 1 Center 面板视觉表达不够直观
M3: SJ 公式(1)符号首次出现时不够清晰
M4: "correlation-selection gap" 在 Abstract 首次出现缺直觉解释
M5: Appendix 33 页建议加目录
M6: 引用验证发现 4 个 minor first-author name mismatches

## 7. Overall Assessment
**Overall**: 3.5 / 5（Borderline Conference）
**Soundness**: 3.5 / 5
**Excitement**: 3.5 / 5
**Reproducibility**: 4 / 5
**Confidence**: 4 / 5

**一句话总评**: 实验极为扎实的 diagnostic study，方法论有贡献，但 SciERC 单 seed、理论 bound 过松、RE/SQL 单薄、degeneracy 可能是主要解释，使其停留在 borderline conference。补充 SciERC 多 seed 并收紧理论定位有望 clear accept。

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责**新颖性 + 相关工作完整性 + 写作/呈现质量**。

## Venue 信息
- **EMNLP 2026** via ACL Rolling Review
- ARR submission deadline: 2026-05-25
- Long paper: 正文 8 页 + 无限参考文献页
- Limitations/Ethics 不计入页数
- 模板: ACL LaTeX (review mode)
- 匿名: Double-blind
- Overall Assessment 评分: 5=Award, 4=Conference, 3=Findings, 2=Resubmit, 1=Do not resubmit

## 论文路径
- LaTeX: `docs/paper/main.tex`（各 section 在 `docs/paper/sections/` 下）
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.pdf`（47页，正文8页+Appendix）

## 你的审查侧重

### 1. 新颖性评估
- "correlation-selection gap" 作为核心概念的新颖性——是否只是已知现象的重新包装？
- 四阶段 diagnostic cascade 相比现有 SC failure mode 分析（Chen et al. 2024a, Huang et al. 2024）的增量贡献？
- 与 Yun et al. (2025) "diversity collapse" 的区分是否充分？两者是否本质上在说同一件事？
- 与 Yao et al. (2026) consistency-accuracy correlation 的关系——本文的 diagnostic 是否只是 Yao 的负面案例？
- Entity construction via MBR 的方法论新颖性——MBR decoding 在 MT/code 中已有大量工作，NER 应用是否有足够的 novelty？
- 正面结果（+0.3-1.4pp）的 practical significance——这个幅度是否值得一篇长文？

### 2. 相关工作完整性
- 检查 Related Work (Section 8) 是否遗漏重要文献
- 特别关注：test-time compute scaling, structured prediction, NER, self-consistency, MBR decoding, uncertainty estimation 等领域的最新进展（2025-2026）
- 用 web_search 搜索可能遗漏的近期工作

### 3. 写作与呈现质量
- Introduction 的 narrative flow 是否清晰？reader 能否快速理解核心问题和贡献？
- 8 页正文 + 37 页 Appendix 的比例是否合理？是否有关键信息被不当地放入 Appendix？
- Figure 1 (overview) 的信息密度和可读性
- 表格设计（Table 1-5）的清晰度
- 术语一致性（degeneracy, alignment, compression 等）
- 论文结构：10 个 section 在 8 页内是否过于拥挤？
- Abstract 和 Conclusion 是否准确概括了论文的发现？是否有 overclaiming？
- Limitations 的覆盖是否充分？

### 输出格式
请按以下结构输出审稿意见：
1. **Summary**（2-3 句）
2. **Strengths**（逐条）
3. **Weaknesses**（逐条，标注 severity: Critical / Major / Minor）
4. **Questions**（需要作者回答的问题）
5. **Missing References**（遗漏的重要相关工作）
6. **Overall Assessment**（评分 + 置信度 + 简要理由）

# ACL Rolling Review - Complete Review Form Rubric

## Overall Assessment
**Scale:** 1-5 (with .5 increments)

- **5:** "Consider for Award: I think this paper could be considered for an outstanding paper award" (top 2.5%)
- **4.5:** Borderline Award
- **4:** "Conference: I think this paper could be accepted to an *ACL conference"
- **3.5:** Borderline Conference
- **3:** "Findings: I think this paper could be accepted to the Findings of the ACL"
- **2.5:** Borderline Findings
- **2:** "Resubmit next cycle: I think this paper needs substantial revisions"
- **1.5:** Resubmit after next cycle (revisions cannot be completed in one cycle)
- **1:** "Do not resubmit: This paper has to be fully redone, or it is not relevant"

## Soundness
**Scale:** 1-5 (with .5 increments)

- **5:** "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen"
- **4:** "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all claims"
- **3:** "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for main claims"
- **2:** "Poor: Some main claims are not sufficiently supported"
- **1:** "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

## Excitement
**Scale:** 1-5 (with .5 increments)

- **5:** "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others and/or attend presentation"
- **4:** "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others and/or make effort to attend"
- **3:** "Interesting: I might mention some points and/or attend if time permits"
- **2:** "Potentially Interesting: does not resonate with me, but might with others"
- **1:** "Not Exciting: does not resonate with me" or lacks relevance to computational linguistics

## Reviewer Confidence
**Scale:** 1-5

- **5:** "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read carefully and am familiar with related work"
- **4:** "Quite sure. It's unlikely, though conceivable, that I missed something"
- **3:** "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something"
- **2:** "Willing to defend evaluation, but fairly li
…[truncated 704 chars]

Based on the web search results, here are the key findings related to your query on self-consistency, structured prediction, NER (Named Entity Recognition), and entity extraction in 2025-2026:

## Self-Consistency in NER

A self-consistency method has been proposed in which prompts with inconsistent predictions are discarded. This unsupervised training technique for NER centered around cycle consistency showed that the accuracy of the model increases with the number of times prompts are changed for entity and context in a sentence.

## Recent NER and Entity Extraction Research (2025-2026)

## Test-Time Scaling Overview

Test-time scaling (TTS) refers to the strategy of trading more computational resources for more predictive accuracy at inference time, and improves the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) while keeping model parameters unchanged.

## Key Techniques and Methods

The Chain-of-Thought (CoT) technique sequentially generates a number of intermediate textual tokens describing the thinking process before predicting the final answer. Recent research has explored various approaches:

- **Parallel Scaling**: Parallel scaling strategies improve performance by aggregating answers across multiple independently sampled reasoning traces, with Self-consistency sampling diverse reasoning paths and choosing the most frequent final answer.
